Now PRIORITY 3 — charter proposal file."}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6,"cache_creation_input_tokens":88381,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":3305,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":88381,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":6,"output_tokens":3305,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":88381,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":88381},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarVTsiv2EiETfAHXWB9n","type":"assistant","uuid":"82cbbeef-328c-421d-860d-0aa70614cbe3","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:04:45.736Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"82cbbeef-328c-421d-860d-0aa70614cbe3","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01B9pR9iTSHsu2tzHx8DZdJV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01N6NaXLCL8DqYsGJMfCQuNY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\proposals\\charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md","content":"---\nstatus: PROPOSAL\nproposal_id: L-S208-1\ncreated_at: 2026-05-09\ncreated_via: S213 main-session opus47-max (Phase 3.5 L-S208-1 charter-tier promotion)\ntarget_file: agent-workspace/constitution/portability.md (or new section in invariants.md)\nratification_path: USER-GATE via Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (pending; M-S173-1 deny holds)\ncompanion_hooks_already_shipped:\n  - scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (Stop hook; auto-detect anti-pattern; 6/6 firing-test PASS at S212)\ncompanion_skill_already_shipped:\n  - .claude/commands/vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine (S213)\nrelated_lessons:\n  - L-S11-1 (Windows portability — pairs with this rule as cross-platform doctrine)\n  - L-S204-1 (verify-before-trust — applied 5x across S208-S212 chain)\n---\n\n# Charter Amendment Proposal — Settings.json Hook Command Portability (L-S208-1)\n\n## Why this proposal exists\n\nThe S208-S212 root-cause investigation surfaced a catastrophic-class harness regression: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) were silently dormant in real-production for 2 days (2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09), undetected by the very harness-health emergency-broadcast signal (HH-1) that was supposed to detect such regressions — because HH-1 itself was the dormant hook.\n\nThe mechanism: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries used **bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax** in their `\"command\":` field — e.g.,\n`\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"`.\n\nThis pattern works in a bash subshell (where firing-tests run, giving false confidence) but **silently fails in Claude Code's Windows hook runtime**: the outer process tokenizes the command string and treats `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart` as a separate command, raising `CommandNotFoundException` (verified via direct PowerShell repro) and never launching the hook.\n\n**Empirical evidence chain (S208-S212)**:\n- S208: 5-fold cross-hook comparison — 3 hooks with inline-env-prefix have ZERO real-production emit lines since 2026-05-07; other hooks (no prefix) emit normally. PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`. `bash -c '...'` wrapper repro succeeds.\n- S209: env-dump diagnostic shipped (no-prefix vs with-prefix vs bashc-wrap variants).\n- S210: diagnostic data confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute; `no-prefix` variant DOES; CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT is NOT natively exported by Claude Code on Windows (falsifies simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix).\n- S211: bashc-wrap variant production-runtime confirmed (CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured) → 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper.\n- S212: production verification at next UserPromptSubmit firing — all 3 previously-dormant hooks emitted `state=GREEN` lines; HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. **Bug fully resolved + auto-detect Stop hook landed.**\n\nThis bug existed for 2 days because:\n1. Firing-tests run in a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env-prefix → false confidence.\n2. HH-1 (the meta-detector for harness regressions) was itself a victim → no emergency broadcast.\n3. No charter-tier rule prevented the anti-pattern at authoring time.\n\n## Proposed charter rule\n\n### Rule: Settings.json Hook Command Portability\n\n> `.claude/settings.json` hook entries' `\"command\":` field MUST NOT begin with bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax `VAR=val command` (e.g., `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash <path>`). This pattern silently fails in Claude Code's Windows hook runtime — the outer process tokenizer does NOT spawn a bash subshell to parse the prefix.\n>\n> When a hook needs a command-scoped environment variable, wrap the entire command in `bash -c '...'` to force bash subshell parsing. Examples:\n>\n> **Anti-pattern**:\n> ```json\n> \"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/foo.sh\\\"\"\n> ```\n>\n> **Correct**:\n> ```json\n> \"command\": \"bash -c 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/foo.sh\\\"'\"\n> ```\n>\n> Or — if the env var has a sensible default in the hook script — drop the prefix entirely and rely on the hook's `EVENT=\"${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-<fallback>}\"` self-detection.\n\n### Auto-detection\n\n`scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` (Stop hook, shipped S212) greps `.claude/settings.json` at every turn-end for the regex `\"command\":\\s*\"[A-Z_][A-Z_0-9]*=` (uppercase-env-prefix anchored at command-string start, deliberately excluding the corrective `bash -c '...'` wrapper which starts with `bash` not an env-name token). State emit: `GREEN` (zero violations) / `WARN-N` (N violations with line-by-line output). Firing-test `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector-fire-test.sh` covers 3 scenarios × 6 assertions, currently 6/6 PASS.\n\n### Skill enforcement\n\n`.claude/commands/vbw-check.md` § `Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability (L-S208-1 subroutine)` (S213) defines a triple-check protocol applied at any spec/test/code/commit checkpoint touching `.claude/settings.json` hook entries: (1) anti-pattern grep, (2) wrap-if-env-needed, (3) JSON validity, (4) firing-test re-run.\n\n## Pairing with L-S11-1\n\nL-S11-1 codifies general Windows portability for hook scripts (line endings, path separators, shebang). L-S208-1 extends that doctrine specifically to `.claude/settings.json` invocation strings — a layer above hook script source. Both rules belong in the same charter section (`portability.md` or equivalent).\n\n## Scope of binding\n\n- BINDING on all future edits to `.claude/settings.json`.\n- BINDING on harness-bootstrap scripts that generate `.claude/settings.json` (e.g., the `attach` skill).\n- ADVISORY for non-Windows-deployment forks (the bug is Windows-runtime-specific; Linux/macOS bash-runtime honors inline-env-prefix), but the wrapped form works on all platforms uniformly so universal adoption is preferred.\n\n## Promotion-readiness checklist\n\n- [x] Hook-tier auto-detect shipped (S212; firing-test 6/6 PASS).\n- [x] Skill-tier enforcement shipped (S213 vbw-check.md extension).\n- [x] Production verification: 3 previously-dormant hooks emitting `GREEN` after fix (S212 12:57:35).\n- [x] Empirical evidence chain documented across 5 sessions (S208-S212) with zero false-fix iterations.\n- [ ] Charter-tier ratification — gated by Cluster C charter Q&A bundle to user (M-S173-1 deny on direct constitution writes).\n\n## Suggested target file\n\nEither:\n- (a) Add new section `## Settings.json Portability` to existing `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` or `invariants.md`, OR\n- (b) Create new `agent-workspace/constitution/portability.md` consolidating L-S11-1 + L-S208-1 + future portability rules.\n\nRecommend (b) — clean home for cross-platform doctrine; L-S11-1 currently scattered across agent-notes.\n\n## Schedule into Cluster C charter Q&A mega-bundle\n\nWhen user signals readiness for next charter Q&A bundle, include this proposal alongside other deferred charter-tier promotions (L-S204-1, L-S207-1, etc.). Single bundle → single decision point → minimum human-in-the-loop friction.\n\nEnd of proposal.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6,"cache_creation_input_tokens":88381,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":3305,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":88381,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":6,"output_tokens":3305,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":88381,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":88381},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarVTsiv2EiETfAHXWB9n","type":"assistant","uuid":"49486925-9a63-4cf5-852d-c84c2ab0cfae","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:05:37.544Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

D-042's mechanism hypothesis (spawn = truncation cause) is REFUTED.\n26\t**Rule**: SCOPE-tier shipped fixes MUST be verified empirically against the SCOPE OUTCOME (the user-visible state change), not just the PROXY MECHANISM (the immediate code reorder). S204 attempted the proposed `/\\\\\\\\$/` fix per checkpoint plan → uncovered 6 false positives (L-S48d-1 violations 2 → 8). Empirical re-verification using `Write` tool (preserves bytes exactly) + `awk -f file.awk` + `od -c` proved the OPPOSITE: original `/\\\\$/` (2 backslashes literal in awk source → regex `\\\\` = literal `\\` + EOL `$` anchor) IS CORRECT and DOES match `\\` at EOL. S203 diagnostic was misled by Bash-tool I/O layer that double-strips backslashes through inline single-quoted commands. L-S203-1 framing is FALSE; reverted + retired with prejudice.\n36\t**Rule**: When a checkpoint/lesson claims a regex (or any escape-laden code) behaves wrongly, verify EMPIRICALLY before acting: (1) write minimal awk via `Write` tool to a file (preserves exact bytes); (2) run via `awk -f file.awk`; (3) verify byte content via `od -c`. NEVER trust bash inline `awk '...'` for diagnostic purposes — bash + tool-shim quoting strips backslashes. L-S201-1 (Guardian-needs-calibration) applies recursively to ALL memory artifacts including checkpoints + lessons.\n37\t**Anti-example**: S203 ran `echo 'X=$(foo)' | awk '$0 ~ /\\\\$/' && echo MATCH` via Bash tool (strips backslash count) → got false MATCH → concluded regex broken → wrote checkpoint claim → S204 acted on claim without re-verification → 4× the violation count emerged.\n38\t**Correct example**: S204 used `Write` tool to author `/tmp/test-2bs.awk` with literal `/\\\\$/` (verified via `od -c` showing 2 backslash bytes) + `awk -f` it on actual file → confirmed correct match → reverted regression cleanly (39/39 firing tests PASS + lint back to 11 violations baseline).\n39\t**Severity**: critical\n40\t**Auto-detect**: maybe — pre-flight check at any `Edit` to a hook script that contains `awk '/\\\\` or similar escape-dense regex: warn agent to use `Write` + `awk -f` + `od -c` triple-verify before commit.\n41\t**Promotion target**: skill (verify-regex-empirically subroutine in vbw-protocol) + charter doctrine entry (cheapest-by-RISK applies to memory-trust too — verify before commit even when memory says \"fix is X\"). NO hook fix needed (production regex is correct as-is).\n42\t**Promotion status (S205, 2026-05-09)**: SKILL-TIER LANDED — inline `Verify-Regex-Empirically (L-S204-1 subroutine)` section added to `.claude/commands/vbw-check.md` (~30 LOC); covers Write+od-c+awk-f triple-verify procedure with anti-example + correct-example. Charter-tier promotion DEFERRED (gated by M-S173-1 deny + Cluster C Q&A bundle still pending). Hook auto-detect (PreToolUse Edit pre-flight on awk-regex hook scripts) DEFERRED — wait for 2nd recurrence per cheapest-by-RISK doctrine.\n43\t\n44\t### 2026-05-09 (S201): Guardians need calibration too — verify Guardian claim against ground-truth code BEFORE acting — L-S201-1\n45\t**Context**: S201 entered with S200 checkpoint plan to fix \"3 hook bug classes\" identified via grep pass + bash-hook-lint output. L-S200-1 (just-minted) said \"inspect Guardian BEFORE LLM hypothesis cycle\". S201 applied L-S200-1 to S200's own output — verbatim re-read of 5 affected scripts revealed: (a) effort-escalation guard at line 33-34 actually accepts both UserPromptSubmit AND PreToolUse+Agent (S200 misread as MIS-WIRED); (b) idle-escape + phase-coherence + adr-staleness ALREADY had L-S108-1 date-hour-bucket fix — bash-hook-lint Check 6b regex `\\$\\{?[A-Z_]` matched `${BUCKET}` (the FIX) as if it were the BUG → 3 false-positive flags emitted at every Stop since S195, distracted 13-cycle hypothesis hunt; (c) only Bug Class 3 (HH-1 empty-SID) was a real bug.\n46\t**Rule**: When acting on Guardian (lint, drift-signal, hook-firing analysis) output, MUST verbatim-read the source code Guardian flagged before classifying as bug + before applying fix. Guardian regex/heuristic itself can have false positives; \"Guardian-first\" doctrine pairs with \"ground-truth-first\" verification.\n47\t**Anti-example**: Trust S200 checkpoint's \"3 bug classes\" framing + dispatch fixes per checkpoint plan without verifying each framing against the actual script. S200 itself trusted bash-hook-lint output verbatim across 13 hypothesis cycles without verifying the lint regex was correct.\n48\t**Correct example**: S201 read all 5 scripts verbatim (5 parallel Read calls) BEFORE any Edit. Caught 2 of 3 framings as misdiagnosis. Found Guardian-itself bug. Fixed real bug + Guardian regex bug in single session (~21 LOC across 2 files).\n49\t**Severity**: high\n50\t**Auto-detect**: yes — bash-hook-lint Check-N addition: detect when an Edit/Write tool call references a hook flagged in latest lint output WITHOUT a paired Read tool call on that hook in same turn.\n51\t**Promotion target**: hook (Check-N in bash-hook-lint.sh) + skill (verify-Guardian-claim subroutine in vbw-protocol).\n52\t\n53\t### 2026-05-09 (S201): Verify detection signal against producing script — L-S201-2\n54\t**Context**: S187..S200 spent 13 hypothesis cycles on a \"`.effort-escalation.log` MISSING = SILENT\" detection signal. S201 verbatim-read effort-escalation-detector.sh confirmed the script never writes to that log file (only stderr at line 108). The log MISSING was always EXPECTED, not a SILENT signal. okens":1,"output_tokens":2381,"cache_read_input_tokens":91751,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3414,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3414},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarVYVpvYWfpDjE3U1MM3","type":"assistant","uuid":"aca995ff-40d1-44e6-bc5c-421958d0b9db","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:05:49.102Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"aca995ff-40d1-44e6-bc5c-421958d0b9db","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_016CAP6ohoUAnHouikgUxut5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017e6Sq7M1FBL7LtKvyb8oGj","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S207): SCOPE-tier shipped fix MUST be verified empirically against SCOPE outcome — L-S207-1","new_string":"## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S208): Bash-inline-env-var-prefix in settings.json hook commands fails on Windows hook runtime — L-S208-1\n**Context**: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) at `.claude/settings.json` lines 200, 204, 208 + 281, 285, 289 used `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=<event> bash \"<path>\"` inline-prefix syntax. ALL three had ZERO real-production emit lines from 2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09 (2 days dormant) — including HH-1, the harness-health emergency-broadcast signal that was supposed to detect such regressions. Firing-tests passed (false confidence) because they invoke from a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env syntax. Empirical chain S208-S212: PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`; bash subshell repro succeeds; env-dump diagnostic confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute and `bashc-wrap` variant DOES (with CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured); 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper at S211; production verification at S212 12:57:35 — all 3 hooks emitted `state=GREEN`, HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. Bug fully resolved across 5 sessions, zero false-fix iterations.\n**Rule**: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries' `\"command\":` field MUST NOT begin with bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax `VAR=val command`. The Claude Code Windows hook runtime tokenizes the command string WITHOUT spawning a bash subshell to parse the prefix, so the hook never launches. When a hook needs a command-scoped env var, wrap the entire command in `bash -c '...'` to force bash subshell parsing. Note: Claude Code does NOT natively export CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT on Windows (verified at S210 via env-dump) — falsifies the simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix when downstream code branches on EVENT.\n**Anti-example**: `\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"` — silently dormant on Windows; firing-test still passes.\n**Correct example**: `\"command\": \"bash -c 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"'\"` — outer process invokes bash explicitly; inline-env-prefix parsed by bash subshell.\n**Severity**: critical (HH-1 emergency-broadcast signal silently non-emitting for 2 days; the meta-detector for harness regressions was itself the regression victim; D-042 SCOPE-tier fix premise refuted; full investigation cost ~5 sessions across S208-S212).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — `scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh` (Stop hook, shipped S212) greps settings.json for regex `\"command\":\\s*\"[A-Z_][A-Z_0-9]*=` at every turn-end. Firing-test 6/6 PASS.\n**Promotion target**: hook (LANDED S212 + firing-test) + skill (LANDED S213 § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine in vbw-check.md) + charter (proposal at `agent-workspace/proposals/charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md`; pairs with L-S11-1 in proposed `portability.md`).\n**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: HOOK-TIER LANDED at S212 (settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh + firing-test 6/6 PASS, wired into Stop chain); SKILL-TIER LANDED at S213 (vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability ~30 LOC); CHARTER-TIER PROPOSAL written to proposals/ — gated by Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (M-S173-1 deny holds direct constitution writes).\n**Connection to D-042 falsification**: D-042 (S184) reordered continue-injector-spawn idx 18 LAST under the assumption \"spawn-induced chain truncation suppresses HH-1\". Real-production verification at S207 falsified that mechanism: idx 18 fired but idx 17 didn't, proving hooks run in parallel (not sequentially) and the actual cause was the inline-env-prefix at idx 17 itself. D-042 = \"treat the symptom under wrong mental model\" failure (L-S207-1 origin). L-S208-1 = corrected RC. ALL three had ZERO real-production emit lines from 2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09 (2 days dormant) — including HH-1, the harness-health emergency-broadcast signal that was supposed to detect such regressions. Firing-tests passed (false confidence) because they invoke from a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env syntax. Empirical chain S208-S212: PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`; bash subshell repro succeeds; env-dump diagnostic confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute and `bashc-wrap` variant DOES (with CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured); 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper at S211; production verification at S212 12:57:35 — all 3 hooks emitted `state=GREEN`, HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. `hook_codified=NO`); AP-23 + AP-24 are anti-patterns living in `patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` (AP-23) + `agent-notes.md` (AP-24, line 51).\n34\t\n35\t## Cluster identification\n36\t\n37\tLexical and semantic clustering across the 5 candidates reveals 3 cohesive clusters:\n38\t\n39\t### Cluster A — \"Guardian-output-inspection\" doctrine (3 candidates)\n40\t**Members**: L-S200-1, L-S201-1, AP-24 (paired with L-S200-1 per agent-notes.md line 58: `Promotion target: paired with L-S200-1`).\n41\t\n42\t**Why clustered**: All three encode the same recurrence loop — when a deterministic Guardian (lint hook, drift signal, Stop-hook diagnostic) emits a root-cause annotation, the LLM-driven hypothesis exploration over-ran without inspecting the Guardian output. L-S200-1 = \"inspect before next hypothesis\"; AP-24 = the anti-pattern label for the over-run; L-S201-1 = the recursive pair (inspect Guardian output AND verify Guardian claim against ground-truth code). Combined instance evidence: 13 hypothesis cycles S187..S200, applied successfully at S201.\n43\t\n44\t### Cluster B — \"Detection-signal-validation\" doctrine (1 candidate, but high-impact)\n45\t**Members**: L-S201-2 (agent-notes.md lines 33-40).\n46\t\n47\t**Why solo-cluster**: Distinct from Cluster A. Cluster A is about WHAT-to-inspect (Guardian output already exists, must be read). Cluster B is about WHETHER-the-detection-signal-itself-is-valid (the producing script must be verified to actually emit the signal). The 13-cycle phantom-chase at S187..S200 had TWO root causes: (1) ignored Guardian output [Cluster A]; (2) wrong detection signal [Cluster B]. Both surface at the same incident but factor differently — they admit different deterministic checks.\n48\t\n49\t**Note on min-cluster-size**: SKILL spec § Anti-Patterns says \"wait for cluster of ≥3 instances\" — but L-S201-2 cites 13 cycles of phantom-chasing as instance evidence within a single recurring symptom. Treating the 13-cycle hunt as one episode would give cluster-size=1; treating each false-positive cycle as evidence gives 13. Per SKILL anti-pattern guidance (\"Single drift events are noise\") the conservative read is \"wait for second incident\" — but evidence weight justifies promotion now given paired Cluster A. Documented as evidence gap below.\n50\t\n51\t### Cluster C — \"Cheapest-by-RISK\" doctrine (1 candidate, charter-tier)\n52\t**Members**: L-S189+-1 (AP-23 doctrine, 8-instance count post-S201).\n53\t\n54\t**Why solo-cluster**: AP-23 is a meta-rule about WHEN to use deterministic vs LLM Guardian. It's strategically distinct from Clusters A/B (which are tactical \"check Guardian output\"). AP-23 is the rationale behind both — but it's already enshrined in `patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` AP-23 row + `architecture.md` constitution + `promote-rule/SKILL.md` § Purpose (\"hook FIRST\"). 8-instance count means it has earned charter promotion candidacy. Cluster size = 1 here, but the rule itself is referenced by 8 different decision points, satisfying min-cluster-size via referential count.\n55\t\n56\t---\n57\t\n58\t## Per-candidate promotion proposal\n59\t\n60\t### Candidate 1 — Cluster A: Guardian-output-inspection\n61\t\n62\t**Synthesized prevention rule**: When a deterministic Guardian (bash-hook-lint, drift-signal, Stop-hook diagnostic notification) emits a root-cause annotation for a script being modified, the agent MUST verbatim-read the Guardian output AND verbatim-read the flagged source code before authoring an Edit/Write to that script. The Guardian's claim itself requires ground-truth verification (Guardian regex/heuristic can have false positives — see L-S201-1 false-positive on `${BUCKET}` whitelist).\n63\t\n64\t**Q-E3 priority chosen**: HOOK (Check-N addition to `bash-hook-lint.sh`) — paired with skill enhancement (vbw-protocol.md) at lower priority.\n65\t\n66\t**Rationale**: Deterministically checkable. `hook_codified=NO`); AP-23 + AP-24 are anti-patterns living in `patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` (AP-23) + `agent-notes.md` (AP-24, line 51).\n\n## Cluster identification\n\nLexical and semantic clustering across the 5 candidates reveals 3 cohesive clusters:\n\n### Cluster A — \"Guardian-output-inspection\" doctrine (3 candidates)\n**Members**: L-S200-1, L-S201-1, AP-24 (paired with L-S200-1 per agent-notes.md line 58: `Promotion target: paired with L-S200-1`).\n\n**Why clustered**: All three encode the same recurrence loop — when a deterministic Guardian (lint hook, drift signal, Stop-hook diagnostic) emits a root-cause annotation, the LLM-driven hypothesis exploration over-ran without inspecting the Guardian output. L-S200-1 = \"inspect before next hypothesis\"; AP-24 = the anti-pattern label for the over-run; L-S201-1 = the recursive pair (inspect Guardian output AND verify Guardian claim against ground-truth code). Source rules in agent-notes.md: lines 24-31 (L-S201-1), 42-49 (L-S200-1), 51-58 (AP-24). Combined instance evidence: 13 hypothesis cycles S187..S200, applied successfully at S201.\n\n### Cluster B — \"Detection-signal-validation\" doctrine (1 candidate, but high-impact)\n**Members**: L-S201-2 (agent-notes.md lines 33-40).\n\n**Why solo-cluster**: Distinct from Cluster A. Cluster A is about WHAT-to-inspect (Guardian output already exists, must be read). Cluster B is about WHETHER-the-detection-signal-itself-is-valid (the producing script must be verified to actually emit the signal). The 13-cycle phantom-chase at S187..S200 had TWO root causes: (1) ignored Guardian output [Cluster A]; (2) wrong detection signal [Cluster B]. Both surface at the same incident but factor differently — they admit different deterministic checks.\n\n**Note on min-cluster-size**: SKILL spec § Anti-Patterns says \"wait for cluster of ≥3 instances\" — but L-S201-2 cites 13 cycles of phantom-chasing as instance evidence within a single recurring symptom. Treating the 13-cycle hunt as one episode would give cluster-size=1; treating each false-positive cycle as evidence gives 13. Per SKILL anti-pattern guidance (\"Single drift events are noise\") the conservative read is \"wait for second incident\" — but evidence weight justifies promotion now given paired Cluster A. Documented as evidence gap below.\n\n### Cluster C — \"Cheapest-by-RISK\" doctrine (1 candidate, charter-tier)\n**Members**: L-S189+-1 (AP-23 doctrine, 8-instance count post-S201).\n\n**Why solo-cluster**: AP-23 is a meta-rule about WHEN to use deterministic vs LLM Guardian. It's strategically distinct from Clusters A/B (which are tactical \"check Guardian output\"). AP-23 is the rationale behind both — but it's already enshrined in `patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` AP-23 row + `architecture.md` constitution + `promote-rule/SKILL.md` § Purpose (\"hook FIRST\"). 8-instance count means it has earned charter promotion candidacy. Cluster size = 1 here, but the rule itself is referenced by 8 different decision points, satisfying min-cluster-size via referential count.\n\n---\n\n## Per-candidate promotion proposal\n\n### Candidate 1 — Cluster A: Guardian-output-inspection\n\n**Synthesized prevention rule**: When a deterministic Guardian (bash-hook-lint, drift-signal, Stop-hook diagnostic notification) emits a root-cause annotation for a script being modified, the agent MUST verbatim-read the Guardian output AND verbatim-read the flagged source code before authoring an Edit/Write to that script. The Guardian's claim itself requires ground-truth verification (Guardian regex/heuristic can have false positives — see L-S201-1 false-positive on `${BUCKET}` whitelist).\n\n**Q-E3 priority chosen**: HOOK (Check-N addition to `bash-hook-lint.sh`) — paired with skill enhancement (vbw-protocol.md) at lower priority.\n\n**Rationale**: Deterministically checkable. The pattern is: an Edit/Write tool call references a script that appears in the most-recent `bash-hook-lint-warn` notification, WITHOUT a paired Read tool call on that script in same turn-window. PreToolUse hook can guard this. This is exactly the AP-23 \"hook FIRST\" pattern.\n\n**Proposed implementation sketch**:\n- File: `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` — add Check 11 (after Check 10 at line 401)\n- Cross-script: NEW PreToolUse hook `scripts/hooks/guardian-output-inspect-first.sh` (~50 LOC) registered in `.claude/settings.json` PreToolUse matcher `Edit|Write`\n- Logic:\n  1. Read latest `human-workspace/notifications/*-bash-hook-lint-warn.md` (sorted-by-mtime, head 1)\n  2. Extract flagged hook basenames via grep\n  3. If pending Edit/Write `file_path` matches a flagged basename, query session-hooks.log for any Read tool call on same basename in last N turns (N=5)\n  4. If Read MISSING → emit warning to stderr (NOT block; AP-23 hook-tier == warning, not hard block — keeps autonomous-full unobstructed)\n- LOC estimate: ~50 (new hook script) + ~15 (Check 11 in bash-hook-lint to detect missing-paired-Read at lint time)\n- Total: ~65 LOC across 2 files\n- Risk: LOW. New hook + new lint check; both reversible by reverting 2 files.\n- Reversibility: HIGH — single git revert.\n\n**Source rule line citations**:\n- L-S200-1 — agent-notes.md L42-49 (`Promotion target: hook (Check-N in bash-hook-lint.sh) + skill (Guardian-output-inspect-first subroutine) + charter (if recurrence persists)`)\n- L-S201-1 — agent-notes.md L24-31 (`Auto-detect: yes — bash-hook-lint Check-N addition: detect when an Edit/Write tool call references a hook flagged in latest lint output WITHOUT a paired Read tool call on that hook in same turn`)\n- AP-24 — agent-notes.md L51-58 (`Auto-detect: yes — same hook as L-S200-1`)\n\n**Confidence**: HIGH — 3 paired source rules all explicitly name \"hook (Check-N in bash-hook-lint.sh)\" as promotion target; auto-detect=yes on all three.\n\n**Capability-map touch**: `task_class=GUARDIAN_OBS` (new — Guardian-output-inspection cycle).\n\n---\n\n### Candidate 2 — Cluster B: Detection-signal-validation\n\n**Synthesized prevention rule**: When a hook detection signal table relies on file-existence (e.g., `.<hook>.log MISSING → SILENT`), agent MUST grep the producing hook script for matching write sites BEFORE classifying. If hook script has no matching write site, the file-existence signal is invalid as evidence — replace with stderr capture or process-trace.\n\n**Q-E3 priority chosen**: HOOK (deterministic pre-flight checker).\n\n**Rationale**: Per L-S201-2 explicit text (agent-notes.md L39): \"Auto-detect: yes — pre-flight checker that, given a hook detection signal table, validates each `<file>` MISSING/PRESENT signal against grep of the producing hook script.\"\n\n**Proposed implementation sketch**:\n- File: `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` — add Check 12 (after Check 11 above)\n- Logic: scan all `scripts/hooks/*.sh` AND `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/*.sh` for grep patterns matching `\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.log` (file-existence detection on hook log files); for each detected log path, verify the producing hook actually has a write site (`>> \"$LOGFILE\"` / `tee` / `echo > \"$LOGFILE\"`); if NO write site found → emit warning citing L-S201-2.\n- Alternative landing: instead of bash-hook-lint Check 12, scaffold standalone `scripts/hooks/detection-signal-validator.sh` (~70 LOC) firing on UserPromptSubmit (heaviest load already at UserPromptSubmit; cheap to add).\n- LOC estimate: ~30 (Check 12 inline in bash-hook-lint) OR ~70 (standalone) — Check 12 inline is cheaper\n- Risk: LOW-MEDIUM — false-positive risk for hooks that emit logs via path constructed at runtime (string concat). Mitigation: only flag if grep pattern is literal string AND no write site found (skip dynamic paths).\n- Reversibility: HIGH — single revert.\n\n**Source rule line citations**:\n- L-S201-2 — agent-notes.md L33-40\n\n**Confidence**: MEDIUM — single source rule; the SKILL anti-pattern \"wait for cluster of ≥3 instances\" is partially violated (only 1 lesson, but 13 instances within the lesson). Mitigated by paired-with-Cluster-A status.\n\n**Capability-map touch**: `task_class=GUARDIAN_OBS` (shared with Cluster A).\n\n---\n\n### Candidate 3 — Cluster C: AP-23 Cheapest-by-RISK doctrine\n\n**Synthesized prevention rule**: Promotion target ordering for any new learned rule MUST follow AP-23: deterministic hook first (cheapest enforcement, instant feedback), procedural skill second (LLM-codified judgment), charter rule last (invariant + human approval gate). At decision time for any \"where does this rule land?\" question, default to HOOK; only escalate to SKILL when judgment required, only escalate to CHARTER when invariant required.\n\n**Q-E3 priority chosen**: CHARTER (rule encodes invariant about *all* future promotion decisions).\n\n**Rationale**: AP-23 is already partially codified in:\n- `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` (referenced)\n- `.claude/skills/promote-rule/SKILL.md` § Purpose (referenced)\n- `.claude/skills/decompose-work/references/classification-heuristics.md` (referenced)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` AP-23 row\n\nBut these are scattered. With 8-instance count post-S201, the doctrine deserves a single canonical home in `agent-workspace/constitution/` (e.g., `karpathy-principles.md` § new section \"Cheapest-Competent-Tool\" OR a new `cheapest-by-risk.md` constitution file).\n\n**Proposed implementation sketch**:\n- **HUMAN-APPROVAL-NEEDED FLAG** — per CLAUDE.md hard rule, constitution writes require explicit human approval. M-S173-1 deny-lift mechanism gap STILL active (per S201 checkpoint hard locks: `M-S173-1 deny holds: NO constitution writes`).\n- Recommended action: do NOT auto-promote to charter. Instead:\n  1. File a Q-E3.b bundle in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/` requesting explicit user-gate approval to (a) author canonical AP-23 charter file, (b) lift M-S173-1 deny temporarily for the constitution write\n  2. If approved: write `agent-workspace/proposals/cheapest-by-risk.md` (~40 LOC), then mv to `agent-workspace/constitution/` per Track 7 protocol\n  3. Update cross-references in promote-rule/SKILL.md + decompose-work/references + SYNTHESIS.md to point to canonical home\n- LOC estimate: ~40 (new charter file) + ~10 (cross-reference edits in 3 existing files) = ~50 LOC\n- Risk: HIGH if auto-executed (violates M-S173-1 + CLAUDE.md hard rule). LOW if gated through Q&A bundle first.\n- Reversibility: MEDIUM — charter once committed has higher inertia, but explicit revision protocol exists.\n\n**Source rule line citations**:\n- AP-23 — `patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` L153\n- 8-instance count — S201 checkpoint § \"Drift watch\" L64\n- Already-cited locations: promote-rule/SKILL.md L11, decompose-work/references/classification-heuristics.md, architecture.md\n\n**Confidence**: HIGH on doctrine; LOW-MEDIUM on charter promotion timing (M-S173-1 deny holds).\n\n**Capability-map touch**: `task_class=PROMOTION_PRIORITY` (governance-tier).\n\n---\n\n## Final summary table\n\n| # | Cluster | Candidate(s) | Target | Implementation file(s) | LOC | Order | Confidence | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | A — Guardian-output-inspection | L-S200-1 + L-S201-1 + AP-24 | HOOK (PreToolUse) + Check 11 | `scripts/hooks/guardian-output-inspect-first.sh` (NEW) + `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 11 | ~65 | **1st** (cheapest, highest evidence) | HIGH | Auto-detect explicit in 3 source rules |\n| 2 | B — Detection-signal-validation | L-S201-2 | HOOK (Check 12 inline) | `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 12 | ~30 | **2nd** (cheap, single source) | MEDIUM | Mitigate false-positive on dynamic log paths |\n| 3 | C — Cheapest-by-RISK doctrine | L-S189+-1 (AP-23) | CHARTER (gated by Q&A) | `agent-workspace/proposals/cheapest-by-risk.md` → constitution mv | ~50 | **3rd** (HUMAN-APPROVAL-NEEDED, M-S173-1 blocker) | HIGH on doctrine / LOW-MED on timing | DO NOT auto-execute; file Q&A bundle first |\n| **TOTAL** | | 5 candidates → 3 clusters | 2 hook + 1 charter | 3 files modified + 1 new | **~145 LOC** | | | 2 immediate + 1 gated |\n\n## Recommended execution ordering\n\n1. **Cluster A first** (~65 LOC, immediate): cheapest deterministic; highest evidence weight (3 source rules); explicit auto-detect cited in all three.\n2. **Cluster B second** (~30 LOC, immediate): cheap inline addition; lower evidence weight but logically paired with A (same incident).\n3. **Cluster C third** (gated): file Q&A bundle for charter promotion + M-S173-1 deny-lift; do NOT auto-execute. {"parentUuid":"727df36a-afa8-468e-b131-485426ccab5d","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"edited_text_file","filename":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\current-execution-archive-2026-05-09-S208-to-S208.md","snippet":"1\t# Current Execution Archive — S208 (single-session)\n2\t\n3\t> Archived from `current-execution.md` at S213 close (2026-05-09) per tracking-retention rule (≤5 sessions inline). S208 was the oldest of 6 inline sessions (S213, S212, S211, S210, S209, S208) and the L-S208-1 root-cause investigation entry-point — content preserved verbatim below.\n4\t\n5\t---\n6\t\n7\t## S208 — Phase 3.5 — VERIFY+RC-DONE: HH-1 production non-emission **ROOT-CAUSED**. Cross-hook comparison at 12:08:22 (Hypothesis B preflight per S207 checkpoint) yielded a stronger finding than expected. **D-042's \"chain truncation\" mental model is fully refuted**; new mechanism candidate L-S208-1 identified: 3 SessionStart hooks (idx 15 idle-escape-detector, idx 16 phase-status-coherence, idx 17 harness-health-self-scan) ALL use inline-env-prefix invocation `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=<event> bash \"...\"` in settings.json (lines 200, 204, 208). ALL THREE have zero real-production emit lines after 2026-05-07; firing-tests work because they invoke from within a bash subshell (which honors inline-env syntax). Direct PowerShell reproduction confirms: `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash <path>` raises `CommandNotFoundException` in PowerShell. `bash -c \"<same string>\"` works fine. Strong evidence that Claude Code's Windows hook runtime does not always wrap commands in a bash subshell — at least not for these 3 entries — leaving the inline-env prefix unparsed.\n8\t\n9\t**Pre-state**: S207 close (D-042 falsified at 12:08:22 real-production /clear). S208 PRIORITY 2 (per S207 checkpoint) = re-investigate HH-1 production non-emission via Hypothesis B cross-hook comparison (cheapest preflight).\n10\t\n11\t**Trigger**: User trivial-prompt \"continue\" at 2026-05-09T~12:35+07:00 (S208 mid-session continuation; SessionStart hook fired with sync-grilling-due notification non-blocking).\n12\t\n13\t**S208 VERIFY — 5-fold empirical evidence (per L-S204-1 doctrine; verify before action)**:\n14\t1. **Parallel execution proven**: .session-hooks.log at 12:08:22 shows idx 18 continue-injector-spawn FIRED appears BEFORE idx 2 essential-routing-fields-verifier emit and idx 4 bootstrap parse-check (which appears at 12:08:23-24). Hooks therefore run in parallel, not sequentially — D-042's spawn-position-causes-truncation premise was based on a sequential-execution mental model that never held.\n15\t2. **idx 18 fires while idx 17 doesn't**: continue-injector-spawn (idx 18, no inline-env prefix) emit line PRESENT at 12:08:22; harness-health-self-scan (idx 17, inline-env prefix) emit line ABSENT + cache `.harness-health-cache-unknown` mtime stuck at 2026-05-07T10:46. If chain truncated between 16↔17, idx 18 also wouldn't fire — counterfactual proves chain truncation is NOT the mechanism.\n16\t3. **3-hook pattern correlates with prefix**: grep `^\\[.*\\] harness-health-self-scan: state=` in production log returns 28 lines, ALL 2026-05-07 OR firing-test-smoke-* OR manual-* sessions. Same for idle-escape-detector + phase-status-coherence — only s175-prod-smoke-* emits, no real-session emits since 2026-05-07. Settings.json grep confirms these 3 hooks (and ONLY these 3) use the `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=<event> bash` inline-prefix pattern.\n17\t4. **PowerShell rejects syntax**: ran `powershell -NoProfile -Command \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash 'scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh'\"` → CommandNotFoundException (`The term 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart' is not recognized as the name of a cmdlet, function, script file...`). Exit code 1, hook never launches.\n18\t5. **bash subshell honors syntax**: ran `bash -c 'CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \"scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\"'` → cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09T12:39, state=RED-1 emit appeared in log. Reproduces firing-test success path.\n19\t\n20\t**S208 RC framing**: Claude Code on Windows uses some hook-runtime path (possibly the Windows %ComSpec% / cmd.exe / a custom parser, NOT a uniform bash -c subshell wrapper) that does NOT honor bash-style inline-env-var-prefix syntax for at least these 3 hooks. The marker echo at settings.json line 144 (which uses `$(date -Iseconds)` + `$VAR` expansion) DOES work in production → so SOMETHING bash-compatible IS parsing the strings. Inconsistency: marker line works but inline-env-prefix doesn't. Possible explanations (not yet verified): (a) Claude Code's Windows hook executor invokes commands via cmd.exe /c which interprets `$(...)` via subsequent bash but not inline-env-prefix; (b) a custom Windows parser that handles `$(...)` substitution but not VAR=val command-prefix; (c) PowerShell-as-default with some subset of bash-like accommodations. FAIL|fail|error) OUTCOME='\"FAIL\"' ;;\n    BLOCKED|blocked) OUTCOME='\"BLOCKED\"' ;; *) OUTCOME='\"DONE\"' ;;\n  esac\n\n  # FIFO match: find oldest DISPATCHED for this parent_session_id without a matching COMPLETED.\n  # Falls back to legacy AGENT_ID hex if FIFO returns nothing (e.g. dispatch.jsonl truncated).\n  eval \"$(node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs'),path=require('path');\nconst dispatchPath=process.argv[1], sid=process.argv[2], legacyAgentId=process.argv[3]||'';\nlet tid='', atype='unknown-agent', model='unknown';\ntry {\n  const lines=fs.readFileSync(dispatchPath,'utf8').split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const events=lines.map(l=>{ try{return JSON.parse(l);}catch{return null;} }).filter(Boolean);\n  const sessionEvents=events.filter(e=>e.parent_session_id===sid);\n  const dispatched=sessionEvents.filter(e=>e.event==='DISPATCHED');\n  const completedTids=new Set(sessionEvents.filter(e=>e.event==='COMPLETED'&&e.tool_use_id).map(e=>e.tool_use_id));\n  const unmatched=dispatched.filter(d=>d.tool_use_id&&!completedTids.has(d.tool_use_id));\n  if (unmatched.length>0) {\n    const pick=unmatched[0]; // FIFO oldest\n    tid=pick.tool_use_id||''; atype=pick.agent_type||'unknown-agent'; model=pick.model||'unknown';\n  }\n} catch {}\nconst q=v=>JSON.stringify(String(v||''));\nconsole.log('TID='+q(tid));\nconsole.log('ATYPE='+q(atype));\nconsole.log('MODEL='+q(model));\n\" \"$DISPATCH_JSONL\" \"$PARENT_SID\" \"${AGENT_ID:-}\" 2>/dev/null || echo 'TID=\"\"\nATYPE=\"unknown-agent\"\nMODEL=\"unknown\"')\"\n\n  # HH-B.2: extract tokens_real + duration_ms + failure_mode from subagent transcript JSONL.\n  TOKENS_REAL_JSON=\"null\"; DURATION_MS_JSON=\"null\"; FAILURE_MODE_JSON=\"null\"\n  if [ -n \"${TRANSCRIPT_PATH:-}\" ] && [ -f \"$TRANSCRIPT_PATH\" ]; then\n    eval \"$(node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs');\nconst tp=process.argv[1];\nlet tokens=0, firstTs=0, lastTs=0, fmode='', errCount=0, lineCount=0;\ntry {\n  const lines=fs.readFileSync(tp,'utf8').split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    try {\n      const e=JSON.parse(line);\n      lineCount++;\n      const u=e.message&&e.message.usage;\n      if (u) {\n        tokens+=(u.input_tokens||0)+(u.output_tokens||0)+(u.cache_creation_input_tokens||0)+(u.cache_read_input_tokens||0);","numLines":80,"startLine":95,"totalLines":209}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"280b517b-585b-4b7d-86a6-cb4eded2b8b0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"8f317104-73d5-4119-8e3f-957851e9b5ca","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01XhV1wfZjqZ4pdQMehBqhMV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TDKD2jer7kTZHsqYfSaRnp","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S208): Bash-inline-env-var-prefix in settings.json hook commands fails on Windows hook runtime — L-S208-1","new_string":"## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S213): Per-session dispatch-pending sidecar must record completion to silence HH-6 false-positives — L-S213-1\n**Context**: HH-6-DISPATCH-STALE=MEDIUM(stale=1) fired in S213 UserPromptSubmit hook. Empirical L-S204-1 verify-before-trust: stale sidecar `.dispatch-pending-1ed5fa47-...jsonl` tail-1 = `{\"state\":\"pending\"}` for dispatch_id `toolu_015e7x898N6k6Ed1yJvmTgKe`, BUT `dispatch.jsonl` line 240 confirms same dispatch_id COMPLETED+DONE at ts_ms=1778299773684 (3.5min after PreToolUse). Bug: `scripts/hooks/dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh` SubagentStop branch (line 199-204) appends COMPLETED to global `dispatch.jsonl` but NEVER updates per-session sidecar. Sidecar accumulates \"pending\" entries forever. HH-6's `tail -1 == state=pending` heuristic flags after 2hr → systemic false-positive. D-041 (S181, 2026-05-07) batch-cleaned sidecars but did NOT fix structural cause; S213's stale=1 = 2nd recurrence (AP-23 RED FLAG: \"Refinement-of-rule (lesson-about-lesson) is AP-23 RED FLAG: 2nd instance mandates promote-or-retire\").\n**Rule**: When a hook maintains a per-session state sidecar with lifecycle fields (state=pending/completed/failed), the lifecycle-completion event handler MUST update the sidecar in addition to any global audit log. Otherwise downstream consumers (HH-* checks, observation reports) see stale \"pending\" indefinitely. Pair with: pre-flight cross-check that any new/modified state-tracking hook covers ALL lifecycle transitions, not just open-state.\n**Anti-example**: dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh PreToolUse appends `state=pending` to sidecar; SubagentStop appends only to dispatch.jsonl (canonical audit) without updating sidecar. Sidecar tail-1 stuck at \"pending\" forever; HH-6 falsely reports stale.\n**Correct example**: SubagentStop branch appends `{\"dispatch_id\":\"...\",\"state\":\"completed\",\"outcome\":\"DONE\",...}` to sidecar after writing COMPLETED to dispatch.jsonl. HH-6's tail-1 check sees the completion line; only genuine pending dispatches (recorder ran PreToolUse but session crashed before SubagentStop) flag stale.\n**Severity**: medium (HH-6 false-positive class; not catastrophic but pollutes harness-health emergency-broadcast signal — degrades calibration of HH-* alerts; fixed at S213).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — firing-test TC5b in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/dispatch-jsonl-recorder-fire-test.sh` asserts sidecar tail-1 contains `\"state\":\"completed\"` post-SubagentStop. Regression-detect via firing-test suite.\n**Promotion target**: hook (LANDED S213 via 1-line addition to dispatch-jsonl-recorder.sh SubagentStop branch + companion firing-test TC5b assertion; suite 12/12 PASS post-fix). Skill/charter promotion NOT needed — single-hook structural bug, fix is local.\n**Promotion status (S213, 2026-05-09)**: HOOK-TIER LANDED (1-LOC fix to recorder + 12-LOC TC5b firing-test). Stale sidecar manually deleted (audit preserved in dispatch.jsonl line 240). HH-6 expected to GREEN at next firing.\n**Connection to D-041**: D-041 (S181) ratified Option C \"backfill-then-clean-sweep\" treating HH-6 stale=6 as a one-time data hygiene issue; did NOT identify the structural cause. S213 RC: D-041 was a \"treat the symptom\" fix; L-S213-1 is the corrected structural prevention. AP-23 cheapest-by-RISK: 2nd-recurrence-class bug ⇒ root fix mandated, not 3rd batch cleanup.\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S208): Bash-inline-env-var-prefix in settings.json hook commands fails on Windows hook runtime — L-S208-1"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":681,"cache_read_input_tokens":177318,"output_tokens":1923,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":681,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1923,"cache_read_input_tokens":177318,"cache_creation_input_tokens":681,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":681},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarWUqjzqEpsCkytyG5qn","type":"assistant","uuid":"e5a1b93d-438a-483e-9a69-2da34f1caf68","timestamp":"2026-05-09T06:18:34.660Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

AP-23 cheapest-by-RISK: 2nd-recurrence-class bug ⇒ root fix mandated, not 3rd batch cleanup.","+"," ### 2026-05-09 (S208): Bash-inline-env-var-prefix in settings.json hook commands fails on Windows hook runtime — L-S208-1"," **Context**: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) at `.claude/settings.json` lines 200, 204, 208 + 281, 285, 289 used `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=<event> bash \"<path>\"` inline-prefix syntax. ALL three had ZERO real-production emit lines from 2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09 (2 days dormant) — including HH-1, the harness-health emergency-broadcast signal that was supposed to detect such regressions. Firing-tests passed (false confidence) because they invoke from a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env syntax. Empirical chain S208-S212: PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`; bash subshell repro succeeds; env-dump diagnostic confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute and `bashc-wrap` variant DOES (with CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured); 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper at S211; production verification at S212 12:57:35 — all 3 hooks emitted `state=GREEN`, HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. Bug fully resolved across 5 sessions, zero false-fix iterations."," **Rule**: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries' `\"command\":` field MUST NOT begin with bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax `VAR=val command`. The Claude Code Windows hook runtime tokenizes the command string WITHOUT spawning a bash subshell to parse the prefix, so the hook never launches. When a hook needs a command-scoped env var, wrap the entire command in `bash -c '...'` to force bash subshell parsing. Note: Claude Code does NOT natively export CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT on Windows (verified at S210 via env-dump) — falsifies the simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix when downstream code branches on EVENT."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e5a1b93d-438a-483e-9a69-2da34f1caf68","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f1f078a3-be02-4a23-b6fb-f8f8cbafb931","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
